</div><h3>Toyota estreia em Tóquio 2019 ultracompacto elétrico</h3>
<p>
    Toyota estreia no Salão de Tóquio 2019 ultracompacto elétrico na versão de produção. Outros BEV (Battery Electric Vehicle) da Toyota, incluindo três “Walking Area” elétricos e Toyota “i-ROAD”, estarão disponíveis para experimentar na Zona “OPEN ROAD” do Salão Automóvel de Tóquio de 2019. 
</p>
<!-- taxonomy-details.nj : end --></a>
</div><!-- spotlights-block-large.nj : end --><!-- spotlights-block-large.nj : begin --><div class="col-xs-12 col-sm-6 element col-xs-12 col-sm-6 element">

<script type="text/javascript">

// Validação da matrícula
function validatePtPlateFormat(tPlate){
	var plate = tPlate.toUpperCase();
	var result = false;
	if (plate!="") {
		result = true;
		if (plate.match(new RegExp('^\\d{2}[A-Z]{2}\\d{2}$'))==null && plate.match(new RegExp('^\\d{2}-[A-Z]{2}-\\d{2}$'))==null)
			if (plate.match(new RegExp('^\\d{2}\\d{2}[A-Z]{2}$'))==null && plate.match(new RegExp('^\\d{2}-\\d{2}-[A-Z]{2}$'))==null)
				if (plate.match(new RegExp('^[A-Z]{2}\\d{2}\\d{2}$'))==null && plate.match(new RegExp('^[A-Z]{2}-\\d{2}-\\d{2}$'))==null)
					if (plate.match(new RegExp('^GNR [A-Z]-\\d{2,4}$'))==null && plate.match(new RegExp('^GNR[A-Z]\\d{2,4}$'))==null)
						if (plate.match(new RegExp('^\\d{3}-[A-Z]{2}\\d{3}$'))==null && plate.match(new RegExp('^\\d{3}[A-Z]{2}\\d{3}$'))==null)
							if (plate.match(new RegExp('^[A-Z]{1,2}-\\d{1,6}$'))==null && plate.match(new RegExp('^[A-Z]{1,2}\\d{1,6}$'))==null)
								result = false;
	}
	return result;
}
	
	function validateSitePlate(){
		var error = '';
		if(!validatePtPlateFormat($('#license_plate').val())){
			error += 'error';
			$('#license_plate').css('border','1px solid #f00');
		} else {
			$('#license_plate').css('border','1px solid #ccc');
		}
		
		plateIsDigit = /^\d+$/.test($('#year').val());
		
		console.log(plateIsDigit);
		
		if(error.length > 0){
			$('#errortextsite').show();
			return false;
		}
		$('#errortext').hide();
		return true;
	}
	
	$(function(){
		$("#vrSubmit").on("click", function (e) {
			if(!validateSitePlate()){
				return false;
			}
		   //e.preventDefault(); // --> if this handle didn't run first, this wouldn't work
		   $(this).attr('href','https://w3.toyota.pt/toyapps/valorizacao-retoma/index.jsp?lp=' + $('#license_plate').val());
		   
		   
		   //$(this).attr('href','https://w3.toyota.pt/toyapps/valorizacao-retoma/index.jsp?lp=' + $('#license_plate').val() + '&model=auris');
		});
	})
	
	</script>
